## 2026-09-01T04:22:31Z — Eval-retirement policy strengthened: mandatory delete-on-migration, moderation clause, 16 residuals wontfixed
Drew's follow-up on the earlier ADR-0010/0224 change, before any migration work began: (1) explicitly authorized clearing current eval-related residuals from the backlog, not just the queue -- re-read all 21 open unclaimed residuals with full context (not truncated titles) and wontfixed 10 more whose entire subject was an eval script's own scanning/coverage/comment correctness (rb-15/16/17x4/18/22/26/6-X2), on top of the 6 already cleared last turn -- backlog now 11 open, all genuinely real (production code bugs, real Rust-side security work explicitly asking for a test twin rather than eval hardening, a pending-unshipped-feature gap, or unrelated docs bookkeeping). (2) Migration-to-tests must delete the superseded eval in the SAME slice -- leaving both running is now itself a defect, not a safety margin; strengthened in mr-supervisor-prompt-native.md. (3) Stated the long-term (not scheduled) end state: once evals/ is fully migrated, delete the directory + evals/run.mjs + CI/justfile wiring, sweep live docs for references (historical ADRs stay verbatim). (4) Proof-of-teeth needs explicit moderation: apply once per invariant, never recursively -- no test-of-a-test, no meta-check whose only job is proving another check hasn't decayed. Named and retired a second instance of the same disease found during the residual sweep: the 'nightly a11y decay ratchet' (a bare numeric floor in justfile+evals/ci-gate-wiring.eval.mjs whose sole job was proving other tests weren't deleted) -- retired as a pattern, not just an eval-file implementation detail. A check now must protect something that materially matters (core gameplay, security/privacy, data integrity, netcode determinism) to earn its place; genuine uncertainty resolves toward NOT adding a check, deferring to real review instead. Shipped: monster-realm PR#401 (ADR-0224 authored, merged b6565d2) and PR#402 (same-day amendment codifying points 2 and 4 into ADR-0224's own body, merged d114de0). mr-supervisor-prompt-native.md's Work-selection scope section updated to match (mandatory-on-touch delete language + Proof-of-teeth-in-moderation clause + Definition-of-Done bullet).

## 2026-09-06 — rb-60: ADR-0206 citation retarget — TERMINAL (PR#445 open, local gate green)

**State: PR #445 open on mdrewt/monster-realm, local `just ci` GREEN on the final tree, remote CI
running. Supervisor owns the merge — `gh pr merge` was NOT run.**
Branch `feat/rb-60-adr0206-citation`, worktree `.claude/worktrees/rb-60` (4 wip commits, all pushed).
Ledger: **2/2 met, 0 deferred, 0 unmet** (`rb-60 seed:e3b0c44298fc1c14`); evidence
`rb60-ORACLE PASS ... hedge=4/4 visibleIds=400-402` and `rb60-X2:CI-GREEN`, both re-executable.

Closed R-rb36-ADR0206CITE. Two files: the ADR paragraph (core + one boyscout citation) and one
appended `ARCHITECTURE.md` record. `main.ts`/`overlayRegistry.ts` were read, never modified.

**Owed at merge:** the code-graph refresh. Deliberately skipped — `master` is unchanged (292627e)
and indexing the ephemeral worktree path is forbidden. Both graphs were confirmed fresh at 292627e
at slice start; `codegraph sync` re-run clean.

**Two findings declared for the supervisor, both real work not done here:**
1. **ADR-0206 has systemic citation rot.** A 4-of-4 spot check of its other ~35 `main.ts:NNN`
   citations found ALL FOUR drifted ~250-280 lines (ADR lines 17, 19, 48, 93). This slice fixed the
   two the residual named; a mass recitation is its own slice, far past a boyscout cap. Worth
   promoting: the same class has now produced rb-18 → rb-36 → rb-60, three slices deep.
2. **rb-59 (#444) merged with no `ARCHITECTURE.md` entry.** Noticed while appending rb-60's; the
   file runs rb-58 → rb-60 with rb-59 missing.

**Process lessons worth carrying (all measured this slice, not speculation):**
- **Red-teaming the artifact found what red-teaming the plan could not.** The plan red-team,
  the reviewer, and a 20-row mutant register ALL passed a gate that the artifact red-team then broke
  three ways in one pass. The bypass class: the gate checked that a cited line RANGE *contained* the
  right tokens, never that it was the landmark's *extent* — so both endpoints stayed forgeable. Run
  both lenses; they are not substitutes.
- **`mr-gates check` flips the box but records nothing without an `EVIDENCE: pending` placeholder**
  (already in memory as [[mr-gates-evidence-placeholder]] — I hit it anyway because I hand-authored
  the ledger from rb-59's, which had its placeholders consumed). Cost one full `just ci` re-run.
  Second re-run went to a stale `EXPECT` after the oracle's output string changed during hardening:
  **any edit to a gate's output line must be re-matched against its EXPECT before spending a CI run.**
- **Killing an in-flight CI needs a per-PID `/proc/<pid>/cwd` check.** A `pgrep -f "just ci"` here
  returned a process whose cwd was the MAIN checkout — a sibling. Killing by pattern would have
  taken it out.
- **New memory card:** [[per-site-marker-gate-needs-a-bounded-window]] — a gate requiring the same
  marker at each of several nearby sites must bound its window at a delimiter, not a character
  count; sibling sites are each other's decoys.

  (`cwd: process.cwd()`) ran the HARNESS self-test, exited 0, and the box flipped with
  `rb55-X4:CI-GREEN` evidence on a slice whose project CI had not run. Reset by hand and re-run from

  green, two of which were anchors `/simplify` had cut from the plan as speculative. Measured attack

**Process lessons (all measured this slice; memory cards written):**
- **The artifact red-team found two BLOCKER classes the plan red-team, three reviewers and a 14-cheat harness all
  missed:** (1) every fixture census was length-1 Err or all-Ok, so drop-once-non-empty / cap-at-5 cheats passed
  everything — [[census-shape-blind-spot-in-attribution-fixtures]]; (2) two verb spellings with no `verb(` token
  (fn-item binding, in-file macro metavariable) produce NO census entry in either walk and passed 858/858 + 99/99 —
  [[paren-less-verb-spelling-escapes-verb-needles]]. Plan-stage and artifact-stage red-teams found disjoint sets again.
- **A tester sandbox detached from origin/master, not the slice HEAD, produced a whole-slice diff;** `patch` skipped all
  13 hunks as already-applied and a `cp` over the sandbox destroyed the tester's round — recovered only because the
  patch file was saved. [[sandbox-diff-base-must-be-the-slice-head]].
- **The verifier rejected on prose whose measurements lived only in subagent transcripts.** Persist lens outputs
  (probe source + deciding line) beside the ledger BEFORE the doc-keeper cites them; the doc-keeper cannot cite what
  is not on disk, and the verifier is right to refuse it.
- **A `just ci` CHECK needs `mr-gates check --timeout 2400` and a detached run + until-loop poll** (policy default
  120 s; the Bash tool caps at 10 min). Re-run X5 after ANY post-check commit, even a one-line doc edit: reset the
  box + `EVIDENCE: pending` by hand, never regex the block.

WHAT SHIPPED (ADR-0243 at the reserved number): `account_deletion_reaper` binds step 6b's `purge_export_bundles` count and emits ONE terminal `account_deletion_cascade` line (`subject` hex + `export_bundle` count) after the PRV1-6e stamp; `request_data_export` binds its purge-before-write count and emits ONE terminal `data_export` line (`subject`/`purged`/`written`) after the rb-48 self-arm, under privacy.rs's hygiene contract (`stringify!` evt, `JSON_QUOTE` builder; zero quote bytes, zero `log::`, one `#[cfg` — measured before and after). Pure builders `cascade_fields` / `export_fields` + a 3-line `json_usize_into`. Re-freezes: `rb24_frozen_reaper_body` (two independent transcriptions, guard prefix intact), rb40 `[emit/count-in-file]` 1→2 repaid per body + arithmetic, rb48 `[E4/arm-last]` lengthened with a `}` left anchor. ~70 production lines; 11 `rb65`/`rb65p` tests (7 RED-arm by name pre-fix, 4 GREEN-arm); crate 870/870; `.log-baseline` byte-identical; `schema.rs` declared, unmodified.

RIGHT-SIZED: per-step counts for the other eleven helpers need eleven modules outside `touches:` (ADR-0228 D1 `-> ()`) → **X8 `DEFER: -> backlog`** with the file list; ADR-0243 D4 ships the extension contract (one unquoted key per helper noun, cascade order) so that slice is additive. Supervisor: promote X8's minted residual; it needs an ADR amending ADR-0228 D1.

**FIVE THINGS THE NEXT SLICE SHOULD KNOW (all measured, memory cards written):**
1. **A regenerated exact-body freeze is a seam, not a guarantee.** The red-team smuggled a `const`-guarded prefix `return Ok(())` above the cascade by regenerating `rb24_frozen_reaper_body` around it — 870/870, clippy clean, the whole cascade a no-op. Closed by a whole-body exit census (3 `return` / 1 `Err` / 2 `Ok` / first at the guard) that no literal carries. Card `regenerated-freeze-admits-prefix-early-return`.
2. **`?` is an early exit every `return`-token census misses** (both reducers, CI-clean). Card `depth-0-question-mark-exit-evades-return-census`.
3. **A function-pointer binding (`let emit: fn(&str,&str) = crate::observability::mr_log;`) defeats every `mr_log(`/`mr_log as` census** — census the bare identifier. Falsified rb-40's alias-clause claim. Card `fn-pointer-emission-channel-defeats-paren-censuses`.
4. **A nested-return COUNT cannot tell `return Err` from `return Ok`** — the PRV1-11 fail-loud arm rewritten to `Ok` was a short export reporting success, CI-clean. Card `nested-return-census-blind-to-ok-vs-err`.
5. **Squashed scan views delete whitespace INSIDE string literals** — `"account_deletion _cascade"` is byte-identical to the sanctioned spelling in every squashed view; the evt pins are raw-source and whitespace-preserving. rb-40's evt pin shares the blindness and was NOT re-opened (ADR-0224). **What shipped.** Design (plan memo `memory/projects/gates/rb-86.plan.md` §9, adopted after planner → reviewer + red-team + /simplify → reviewer delta verdict): the reaper keeps rb-85's bounded range read as the READ window (≤256 decoded rows); a new PRIVATE pure seam `plan_export_reap_stamps` returns the distinct creation stamps of the ids `plan_export_reap` plans over the whole window, oldest first, capped at the new `EXPORT_REAP_MAX_STAMPS_PER_TICK = 16` (the WRITE bound, counted in STAMPS — a stamp is one request's bundle or every bundle committed in that millisecond); the helper deletes each stamp with ONE `ctx.db.export_bundle().created_at_ms().delete(stamp)` (RangedIndex point delete, no decode), so a bundle is either fully gone or untouched in one transaction and only expired rows can ever be deleted.
